## October 28–29 — Codex final integration and independent branch audit

**Scope and ownership.** Behavioral pipeline/data/clinical batch with paired Flutter consumer changes. Sean assigned Codex integration and merges, confirmed Claude editing/autofix paused, and on October 29 reserved the full pipeline run to himself. Baseline main `e923cb233` includes #274. Pipeline #278–#282/#284/#285 are combined in PR #286; source qualification, integration and rebuilt release acceptance are separate.

Owner: `clinical_applicability` exact source/outcome/population/preparation decisions; `evidence_resolver`; `scoring_input_contract` source subjects/roles; `botanical_profile` material specifications; enrichment exposure/UL/interaction projections; existing readiness/publication audit; Flutter consumers. Evidence: matrix/glossary, fail-first production probes, retained raw replays and independent reviews. Will NOT create a second scorer, registry, public semantic field/status, clinical approval or execution register.

- **Accepted clinical policy.** No marketing-claim bonus. Claim-free labels earn only qualified source-, outcome-, preparation- and population-specific Evidence. Vitamin D3, Testofen, lactoferrin and omega benefit holds remain restricted. Existing disclosed EPA/DHA nutrition authority retains its existing 10.4/11.1 magnitudes across routes; it does not assert a heart-event benefit. No retrospective additive Evidence cap was introduced.
- **Testofen pillar independence.** Verified identity/extract/marker specification earns Formulation independently of efficacy; a supplier specification does not certify the retail batch. Exact source/material joins prevent generic aliases and sibling/parent borrowing. The 61-label material comparison has 24 Formulation-only movers and 37 controls, with no other pillar/route/status/safety change. Final combined raw 304641: Formulation 20 (12 neutral identity + 2.6667 extract + 5.3333 specification), Dose 11.4, Evidence 0, Transparency 15, Verification 8, Safety/Hygiene 6; total 60.4, Needs improvement, partial, no_known_catalog_concern. The intermediate material-only 70.8 is not the integrated result.
- **Clinical scope and identity.** Held-source joins, qualified study design/enrollment, signed legacy constraints, explicit eligible booleans, primary versus unknown endpoints and purpose/population attribution share the canonical owner. The 69-record legacy census and signatures remain intact. The 86-label scope comparison has eight Evidence-only movers and no Clean/other pillar/route/status changes. Repeated identical preparations retain distinct verified source refs; constrained, held and underdose cases stay restricted (419 owner checks and 11 independent controls). UC-II is knee-scoped and exact-material bound; Magtein likewise requires its studied material. Original real UC-II 321604 remains a positive regression. Actual 76848 no longer attributes UC-II research to Leucine; its Evidence remains zero.
- **Safety.** Magnesium retains unresolved + review when food/supplemental shares cannot be established; the unsupported universal 10 mg/g food bound was removed. MagnaPower keeps its distinct supplier-specified material without an invented partition. Form-aware pregnancy A/E exposure remains. Sean's bitter-orange ruling removes the unsupported 3 mg severity split and preserves all-dose pregnancy avoid/block, actual synephrine exposure, unknown amounts and independently justified contraindications. Both overlaps and the shared Flutter fixture were checked. Safety evidence includes 212 magnesium checks, 386+3 raw controls, 201 pipeline/28 Flutter checks and 42 products/60 alerts. All 4,434 retained A/E threshold decisions are unchanged by the later uncertainty-preservation repair.
- **Publication owner and California poppy.** Source-bound proof validates current raw→Clean→Enrich→Score lineage and exact clinical/native/nutrition contributors without clearing truthful held Evidence partials or other unresolved dimensions. Missing, ambiguous, stale and inflated credit fails closed. The poppy Dose dependency was resolved through existing literature ownership: powdered-herb/traditional or fixed-combination studies establish no interchangeable benchmark for an unspecified 200 mg extract. No benchmark or clinical approval was invented. Existing neutral Dose changes 62820/62831 from 49.2 to 60.6 total; Evidence stays zero and partial, with three controls unchanged. Source review, primary documents, 413 tests and independent controls are retained.
- **App integration.** PR #164 merged at `cb1ffc39`, reviewed head `ed151752`, required CI 40790156049 green. Existing adolescent pregnancy UL assessments render in Fit/review cards without a second calculation. Canonical app main is clean. This establishes source integration, not a newly rebuilt catalog or installed-app acceptance.
- **Mixture and protein ownership.** Source `715a67aa` reuses the existing complete-mixture provider. A complete source-linked Leucine/Isoleucine/Valine preparation owns BCAA research; isolated, partial, duplicate, foreign and stale compositions cannot. Unknown/bounded amounts cannot become exact doses, and extra members cannot lend parent mass. Amino-acid form text cannot supply whole-whey trial credit; existing all-source protein/caseinate proof remains. Six retained raw fixtures match their original bytes. Original 220827 is protected by its October 1 owner history: separately declared BCAA 5 g with 2.5/1.25/1.25 g children retains its existing qualified soreness credit, while a complete EAA profile alone cannot infer that preparation. Paired replay preserves all pillars and total 60.4, with one EAA 10 g Dose contribution. Source `e893c58b` repairs the further 224676 aggregate-role omission using fresh canonical/source/full-composition/linked-ref proof, restoring Evidence 10.4 without Leucine attribution or a second Dose vote. Its 164 named checks and 16 independent controls passed.

**Checkpoints and test integrity.** Initial candidate `59ee1461` failed CI 40798691147 (25,396 passed, 123 failed, 192 declared skips) and local (542 passed, four failed, 24 declared opt-ins). All failures were classified. Synthetic mechanics fixtures explicitly install test-only source contexts; actual held/unqualified controls remain. The independent 41-path test audit retained its authorship limitations. Forty archetype scalar expectations follow source limitations; the KSM physical-row join and permitted explanation-only config fingerprint amendment do not change numerical policy. Citation identifier boundaries, canonical enrollment lookup, shared omega authority proof and a reference-only material cache were repaired and independently reviewed. No debt baseline was expanded.

Completed runtime `e893c58b` had an independently clear 473-label raw/full/blob replay: 351 changed records, 122 identical, zero Clean/input changes. Only three public numeric movers versus `59ee1461`: mixed-protein 220215/221166 lose unsupported whole-whey Evidence 15.6→10.4, and Sensoril 321944 retains independently justified Formulation 12→14.7. Source ingredients/IQD/amounts/formula/nutrition/interactions/safety/status/routes are unchanged. Eleven RDA/UL wrappers differ only in derived dose-assessment identity/links/exact three-member sums; adequacy and UL flags are unchanged. Seventy Evidence 19–20 cases have unchanged IDs/scores and existing nutrition authority (62 multi/prenatal, eight B panels), with no clinical-contributor receipts. Local: 546 passed/24 declared external opt-in skips in 145.55 s, strict skip guard green. Raw canaries: 33/33. Nine original-hash outputs: seven identical to the reviewed dated baseline; 1036/306237 add only five qualified canonical identity links, with no score/amount/safety change or expanded allowance. Python correctness: zero new/ten existing.

**Second CI checkpoint is failed, not qualified.** Exact candidate `d048fbbe` CI 40829860560 completed with 25,581 passed, four failed and 192 declared skips. Shards 2/3 passed; shards 0/1 failed. The four failures are two real BCAA form-metadata regressions and two UC-II fixture/assumption cases. The real BCAA labels 31148/270253 retain correct enrichment-owned IQM grades 14/15, but fresh composition replacement erased those assessments before scoring. Their original assertions stay unchanged; the source repair must preserve only a current same-source reading, including the actual ratio-note inputs, while revalidating composition and rejecting stale/foreign readings.

Actual raw UC-II 182041 already qualifies. Its minimal positive fixture lacked a source ref; the older title-only generic-cartilage test conflated contextual discovery with a proven material relationship. The corrected tests retain discovery, require exact source qualification and add the byte-identical real raw positive; they do not claim a physician banned title-based proof. Review additionally reproduced an actual discovery loss when UC-II is printed only on the retained ingredient row/forms and the product title is generic. The existing gate now consumes that exact source owner, preserving canonical eligibility and excluding derived, negated and sibling borrowing. Four fail-first cases and 14 focused controls cover this repair. Five synthetic review helpers also used schema-invalid `pooled_synthesis`; fail-first completeness assertions exposed them. Valid, explicitly synthetic review roles and review membership now make the helpers assert complete source review before testing scoring. No production approval was changed.

**Completed repaired source `444a2d8b`.** The form cache now retains the exact existing panel-note matcher input in `notes`; the adapter requires that proof and every current name/form/source/composition/ref input before preserving the four enrichment-owned form fields. Missing legacy proof, changed notes, conflicting readings and foreign/stale compositions remain ungraded until current enrichment. Original 14/15 assertions are intact. The final 293 unique owner/consumer cases and 14 independent source controls passed; all 215 clinical-fixture cases also passed independently. No Score-side IQM rematch, new alias, scoring magnitude, clinical approval or field name was introduced.

Final independent raw/full/blob qualification is clear at `444a2d8ba5ac57740ba650281c348f69086f6d92`: 473 retained labels plus three byte-identical real CI controls, with both captures reporting exit zero and unchanged source. All 473 scored payloads are identical to the independently reviewed `e893c58b` results. The additional metadata records only capture actual panel-note provenance; all 689 retained notes (150 nonempty) match the existing matcher inputs. No cached form grade or clinical record changes. Against `59ee1461`, 395 records change and 78 remain identical; only the three explained public numeric movers remain. All three extra controls retain every pillar and total: UC-II 182041 72.5, BCAA 31148 72.0 and BCAA 270253 64.7. Actual amounts, safety, routes and statuses stay unchanged; original unspecified/ratio grades remain 14/15. The 70 existing high-Evidence cases retain nutrition authority; additional control 31148 is likewise nutrient authority. Final 33/33 raw canaries and all nine original-hash comparisons pass. Python correctness: zero new/ten existing. Receipt: `qualification_retry/independent_movement_review/FINAL_444A_CAPTURE_QUALIFICATION.{md,json}`.

The repaired-source local rung passed 546 tests/24 declared opt-ins in 143.96 s, but a separately started old-main full pipeline job was subsequently discovered in the shared terminal. That makes a stable stored-output checkpoint necessary after the external job stops; its passed run is not being used to conceal concurrent artifact mutation. Sean was asked whether to stop that job because Codex did not start it. Source snapshots use frozen inputs and remain valid. The final whole-fast CI candidate and stable local rerun remain merge gates.

**Operator boundary.** Sean reserved the full pipeline run to himself. After exact-head green CI and the stable local checkpoint, merge and verify every lane's ancestry, preserve the 12 unique local files, fast-forward canonical main, and recheck every stage fingerprint. Existing outputs require regeneration beginning with Clean; live output counts are not treated as a stable census during the external job. No full corpus, catalog publication, newly installed catalog or TestFlight delivery is claimed by this integration batch.

  (`scripts/audits/held_source_closure_20261028/DR_PHAM_RULINGS_20261028.md`) and her attested pre-approval of
  fully source-verified agent contexts (reviewer "Dr. Pham", 2026-10-28, with an agent-authorship limitation).
  Earlier signatures keep their own dates.
- **Result (118 register items + hyaluronic acid).** 70 records complete: 43 with scoped credit, 27 no-credit on the
  reference path with qualified wording. 49 stay held, each with one exact remaining item in
  `clinical_review_holds.csv` and its patch receipt. Whole file: 98 of 210 records complete (from 28).
- **No pending drafts in production data.** Every `source_verified_pending_clinical_review` context (128 on main) is
  approved from a full text or moved to its patch receipt; `test_no_pending_clinical_review_in_data.py` fails on
  main and passes here.
- **Held for a stated reason, not closed:** omega-3 (closing zeroes EPA/DHA products on the generic route; waits for
  the omega nutritional pathway — calibration session), vitamin D3 and Testofen (freshness searches revise the
  conclusion), plant sterols (capsule/tablet full texts unread), cinnamon (expression of concern; weights and
  exclusion sensitivity recorded), PQQ (corrected table unread), lactoferrin (retracted trial; proposed no credit),
  collagen, digestive enzymes, green tea, S. boulardii and abstract-only sources.
- **Measurement.** Frozen 1,111 real labels (prior 495-label cohort + up to six products per register item),
  `~/pg_quality/held_closure_20261028/` (re-measured after merging main 2a55dbcee: `base2.jsonl` on main, `cand7.jsonl` on this
  data, sha256 2dbfe515474b…). 227 Evidence decreases, 0 increases; 1 Formulation change (316118, LJ100 standardization credit
  tied to the misattributed brand); 0 status, safety-gate or dose-safety changes. Drops follow rulings: reference-only
  records (turmeric, milk thistle, HMB, valerian, echinacea…), narrowed scopes (CoQ10 heart failure, ginger nausea,
  white kidney bean Phaseolean, beta-alanine ≥2.4 g/day), whey-only protein credit, one probiotic owner (LGG/BB-12).
- **Integration fixes found while measuring:** source-only label gating rejected real branded labels; whey row-text
  term rejected 100% whey; deleting lifted record holds disabled the lost-context guard (kept as `true`); beta-alanine
  "energy" exclusion over a whole label; berberine credited undosed Oregon grape; inositol outcome term read as
  digestive. All fixed and re-measured.
- **Review.** Independent read-only review: 13 findings; fixed 1–3, 5, 6, 8, 12. Open by decision: condition credit
  without a label claim (Sean decided; needs the shared applicability change, proposed as its own task, with Dr Pham's
  approval of the clarification); population limits only inside the condition gate; held records with no
  applicability still match on the legacy path (pre-existing); Formulation reading clinical status (316118).
- **CI round 1 (a78f78bf9) failed** in files outside the local slice: 19 stale pins (fixed), a renamed test helper
  (fixed), whole-label exclusions matching product names (BCAA, KSM-66: now claim phrases), and UC-II credit moved
  off its brand heading by any applicability block (UC-II keeps main's unscoped policy; heading fix handed to the
  claim-state applicability session).
- **Validation.** After merging main (a51074c1b): clinical slice plus every CI-failed file 4,038 passed; landed
  check 119 records, 0 problems. Python correctness 0 new.

## Cleaner RC2/RC5 batch — PR #184, October 19 (Claude, claude/keen-shirley-de1d6d)

Goal: remove cleaner outputs nothing reads (RC5) and decide nutrition-fact status by nutrient role (RC2), from reports/cleaner_root_cause_audit_20261019. Owners: enhanced_normalizer.py::_is_nutrition_fact/_excluded_row_display_type/_classify_label_source_structure, constants.py::EXCLUDED_NUTRITION_FACTS (LABEL_ROLLUP_NAMES is a computed subset), supplement_taxonomy.py::_is_non_quantified, the RDA/UL owner in the enricher (unchanged). Will NOT create a classifier, normalizer, data file, public field or status. Class: behavioral cleaner/taxonomy change.

Changes:
- RC5: five row fields and labelRelationships removed. entryDate, updatedDate, productVersionCode and offMarket have readers and stay.
- RC2: a %DV-only Calcium/Iron row now goes to the RDA/UL owner as unresolved. This supersedes 12a7a3dcf. Sean, 2026-10-19: "%DV-only calcium and iron — yes, include them in the existing assessment. Do not discard their nutrient identity merely because mass is unavailable. Convert only when the applicable DV basis and conversion are established. Otherwise retain an indeterminate assessment: no guessed mass, no invented UL exceedance and no 'within limits' reassurance. This does not automatically approve every additional interaction message. Review dose-dependent warnings and vague pregnancy/heart-disease messages for actual applicability." Label totals are recorded as summary wrappers. The no-op _FAT_CATEGORY_REAL_ACTIVES is deleted. Only label totals stop counting as a proprietary blend.
- Taxonomy: a %DV-only row is not a quantified dose. This fixes the 16872 casein regression the audit found.
- Warning review (d011e75bb). The 9 products gained 63 warnings across 11 rule x condition types.
  - Calcium/heart_disease is the only dose-dependent one (> 1,000 mg/day). For an unknown amount it showed above-threshold cardiovascular copy. It now shows authored "amount unclear — check with your clinician" copy via enrich_supplements_v3._amount_unknown_copy (threshold *_if_amount_unknown); the disposition stays review.

## October 20 — standardization_note shows only label text (PR201, claude/standardization-note-label-first)

Class: display-only export wording (no calculation, identity, state, contract or eligibility change). Owner:
`build_final_db.py::_compute_standardization_note`, plus its release check `audit_raw_to_final.py::_check_standardization`.
Defect (pre-existing, found on #198): the call site passed `"notes": m.get("notes") or ing.get("notes")`, so a
mapped row's IQM curation notes hid the label's own row notes, and the matched IQM form name was read first. Mapped
rows showed IQM standards as label claims ("organic Turmeric" 3% curcuminoids; "Turmeric root extract" 95%), the label's own
figure was replaced (93% → 3%), and the 11 green tea products #198 releases lost "23–94% EGCG" (Teavigo: None, though
its label notes say 94% EGCG). Fix: label notes, then label name, only; en/em-dash ranges rejected; "total" qualifier and
"epigallocatechin gallate" = EGCG; the audit recomputes from the cleaned label row by raw_source_path.
Checks: fail-first on main for 9 real labels + the 11 #198 products (relationship applied in memory) + dash/wording
cases; 2 stale tests corrected (they called the IQM form name "DSLD-authored"); 15 export/audit/contract files 515 passed,
75 artifact skips. Measurement over the 15,414-product baseline: 699 rows / 567 products (lost 370, all IQM-sourced;
gained 218 and changed 111, all label text). Replay of 567 + 30 controls (`~/pg_quality/standardization_note_pr_20261020`):
the only changed path is `blob:$.ingredients[*].standardization_note`; the updated audit finds 0 dropped claims vs 329 on
main. Flutter lib/ has no reader of this field. Open: DSLD row text on 1627 guarana says "3% ginsenosides" (likely a DSLD
transcription error, shown as recorded).

Formulation moves (854 within 0.5, range -8.1..+2.8), 5 contract canaries refreshed (33/33 check clean), 0 safety changes, and 11 multis scored -> not_scored. Each of the

## October28 — condition-specific evidence: claim and population applicability

**Status:** implemented and measured, not merged. Branch `claude/brave-fermi-d56dfd` (PR #280), rebased onto
main `b1f44d3ee`.

**Rulings:** Dr Pham's rulings were relayed by Sean, who attested them on October 28. Text and attestation are
in `scripts/audits/condition_scope_rule_20261028/DR_PHAM_APPROVAL_REQUEST.md`.
- No claim is required. Credit stays condition-scoped. An unrelated claim earns nothing.
- A recognized but unclassifiable claim resolves conservatively.
- Infant, pediatric, pregnancy and lactation research needs explicit targeting. Other populations fail only on
  an explicit conflict.

**Owner:** `clinical_applicability::assess_clinical_applicability`, using `_assess_condition_scopes`,
`assess_claim_alignment`, `assess_context_population`, `assess_native_claimed_purpose`,
`_excluded_claim_matches` and `preparation_verified_clinical_matches`.
- `evidence_record_matches_declared_purpose` defers to the owner through `condition_scope_governs`.
- The probiotic consumer reads the stamps from `studied_formulas`.
- Formulation reads preparation-verified matches.
- Shared vocabulary lives in `evidence_resolver.INDICATION_KEYWORDS` plus `_INDICATION_PHRASES`.
- Data change: BRAND_UCII's reviewed scope is restored. `data_batch check` passed.

**Tests:**
- Fail-first logs (rounds 1–4) are in `~/pg_quality/condition_scope_20261028/`.
- Focused slice of 76 owner/consumer files, the dead-code ratchet and #277's literature tests: 4,874 passed,
  0 failed, on the main-rebased branch.
- Outcome-level purposes per Sean's PR #278 ruling are included: qualifying outcomes only, and sleep apart
  from stress.

**Measurement** (replay, frozen raw labels, main `b1f44d3ee` vs this branch; identical to the `4bba11a51` pass):
- Main cohort of 460: 48 moved (23 up, 25 down), all Evidence.
- Gated single-ingredient cohort of 40: 20 moved (18 up). Cranberry, ginger, inositol, selenium and riboflavin
  claim-free labels gained their scoped credit.
- No Formulation, Dose, status or safety change in either cohort.
- Causes are in `MEASUREMENT.md`.

**Fresh-context review:** 10 findings. Fixed fail-first: affirmative pregnancy targeting, lactation, title
exclusions, absence/precaution text, unclassified product claims, and exclusion sentences.

**Open for Sean:**
- The probiotic positioning weight pays 16 for an aligned claim and 13 for no claim (D3 vs D1).
- A definition of "qualifying outcome" for pooled reviews: 21 of 61 approved scopes have no primary hierarchy.
- Follow-ups: pre-workout focus/power positioning; record-level study strength (ginger); Evidence owners
  (UC-II on TriFlex 76848, melatonin on 328650); probiotic credit for pooled not_reported outcomes.
